(unalias rg 2>/dev/null; command -v rg) >/dev/null 2>&1; then\" >> \"$SNAPSHOT_FILE\"\n  \n      echo '  alias rg='\"'/root/.nvm/versions/node/v22.22.0/lib/node_modules/\\@anthropic-ai/claude-code/vendor/ripgrep/x64-linux/rg'\" >> \"$SNAPSHOT_FILE\"\n    \n      echo \"fi\" >> \"$SNAPSHOT_FILE\"\n  \n\n      # Add PATH to the file\n      echo \"export PATH=/root/.nvm/versions/node/v22.22.0/bin\\:/usr/local/bin\\:/usr/local/sbin\\:/usr/local/bin\\:/usr/sbin\\:/usr/bin\\:/sbin\\:/bin\" >> \"$SNAPSHOT_FILE\"\n  \n\n      # Exit silently on success, only report errors\n      if [ ! -f \"$SNAPSHOT_FILE\" ]; then\n        echo \"Error: Snapshot file was not created at $SNAPSHOT_FILE\" >&2\n        exit 1\n      fi\n    "
